## Phase 2 finite Milestone drafts — implemented, release pending 23 October 2026

DEV-005 now prepares later invoice Drafts for an actual converted Milestones sale, in the order of its recorded conversion titles and percentages. Accepted quotation amounts provide the finite total; no separate client approval of the structured conversion schedule is inferred. The original first invoice remains unchanged. Accounts/Admin explicitly chooses the sale and later installment, enters actual billing/due dates and reason, and confirms the reviewed source. No milestone completion, automatic billing date, invoice issue, payment or assignment is inferred.

Server authority and exact source are checked under locks. Unique sale/installment and stable request identity prevent skipping, duplicate creation and retries with changed meaning. Earlier invoices must still match their recorded amounts and tax classifications. Changed/voided prior invoices, credits, accepted commercial changes, existing recurrence or incompatible tax remainders require separate review. Final allocation uses the accepted remainder without adding a rounding policy. Immutable evidence retains actual actor, server time, original derivation and manual dates/reason; receipts survive later invoice changes and cannot regenerate a voided installment. UI failures and account/permission changes clear private choices and exports. No production business records or fixtures were inserted.

## Phase 2 finite Advance balance draft — implemented, release pending 23 October 2026

The first bounded DEV-005 increment prepares one later invoice Draft for an actual converted **Advance** sale. It derives the remaining gross/net/tax from the frozen accepted quotation less the original advance, reusing existing monetary precision and tax rates. It is remaining unbilled scope, not the unpaid balance of the first invoice; payments against the first invoice do not reduce it. Actual billing date, due date, reason and confirmation are entered manually, with no preselected dates or recurring cadence. A future-dated draft remains Draft until the existing ordinary invoice workflow acts on it.

Current Accounts/Admin authority is checked and locked/rechecked. Exact reviewed source, one-per-sale uniqueness and stable retry identity prevent changed-source or duplicate generation; immutable evidence records original accepted derivation, actual preparer, server time, dates and reason. The original advance remains untouched. Project association and accepted currency are preserved without FX. Changed/voided advance amounts or tax classification, issued credits and accepted BOQ change offers require separate commercial/payment review. A remaining-tax residual that cannot be represented at the original rate with existing invoice precision also requires review; no new rounding policy is invented. This flow cannot replace a voided balance draft, automatically issue invoices, post payments, adjust WHT, grant assignments, make refunds or deliver external messages. Original creation receipts remain readable even if the ordinary draft is later reviewed/edited/issued/voided.

Focused validation passed: 35 isolated database checks, nine UI checks and existing Finance UI checks; TypeScript/build passed. Full regression/source evidence is recorded in the increment PR. New CLI scaffold `20261023100725_phase_two_advance_balance_drafts.sql` is **UNAPPLIED**, after pending Accounts routing `20261023000245` and customer identity `20261023001126`. Keep the stacked PRs open for coordinated migration/merge/UI release; do not deploy new UI against the old schema or reapply Phase 1 migrations. Held finance 142–148 remains excluded. No live business records, fixtures or assignments were inserted.

DEV-005 is in progress, not complete: later Milestone/Monthly finite payment drafts and broader schedule handling remain. Existing unbounded invoice recurrence was audited and is not repurposed to overbill a finite sale. Next development is the Milestone payment draft increment, then Monthly periods, using actual accepted terms and manual dates; no new billing/working-hour/target policy is inferred. Phase 2 and later phases remain incomplete. Catalogue stays 66 packages, 50 open and 16 development-complete under the existing release-count convention; now 13 in progress and 37 not started. All 41 client-owned acceptance checks and actual Meta connection remain pending. Development continues without waiting for deployment; any eventual live claim requires exact release verification.


## Phase 2 reviewed customer identity — implemented, release pending 23 October 2026

DEV-007 now includes explicit active-Admin customer creation/linking before quotation publication, the necessary prerequisite because accepted publications already freeze a real customer identity. Review shows actual lead details, normalized customer/registered-contact email/phone matches and a separate bounded customer-name search. New customer name stays manual; creation copies only the displayed actual lead email/phone. Existing contact matches block duplicate creation. Shared names alone do not force a match or prohibit an explicitly distinct customer. Conflicting candidates require an explicit reviewed existing-customer choice and reason; existing customer/contact records are never merged or overwritten.

Decisions require exact Deal/lead versions, reviewed current match/target snapshots, actual confirmation/reason and a stable request identity. Directory writes are serialized with match rechecking. Immutable evidence retains actual actor, server time, original lead/link, reviewed matches and selected customer; retries cannot duplicate a customer or change meaning. Changed linkage advances draft quote versions. Current Published/Accepted quotations, closed Deals and converted sales block identity changes; frozen publications are preserved. Quotation publication now requires a current reviewed customer identity instead of silently choosing a customer through the old dropdown. Existing accepted publications/sales are not backfilled or rebound. No user account, client signatory authority, project assignment or external message is created.

Focused checks passed: 38 isolated DB and nine UI checks, with existing quotation/access UI checks retained. TypeScript/build and full regression evidence are recorded in the increment PR. Keep both PRs pending coordinated migration/merge/UI release; do not publish the new UI against the old schema or reapply existing Phase 1 migrations. Finance 142–148 remains held. No actual production business fixtures were inserted.

## Phase 2 Accounts routing — implemented, release pending 23 October 2026

DEV-006 now has an explicit blank/required Accounts recipient in Won conversion. Candidates are actual active staff/Admin accounts with current `finance.manage` authority, queried only for a Deal the actor can currently convert. Conversion rechecks the actor after Deal lock waits and locks/rechecks relevant profile, board, membership, role and team authority. It rejects missing/ineligible/revoked recipients for a new conversion; records immutable recipient/actor/server-time/Deal/sale/first-invoice evidence atomically; and queues a generic in-portal reminder to the authorized Finance workspace, honoring notification opt-out. An exact retry creates no duplicate routing or reminder and cannot change the original recipient. Existing sales receive no backfilled assignment. Routing does not approve/issue an invoice, grant project membership or send external messages.

## Source issues that block direct financial/time imports

- `Project_Management.xlsx`, Project Record N5/O5 sum Invoices F/G, but F is Milestone and G is Invoice Amount; received values belong to H. The same row's X5:AD5 use Expenses H/J, while H is Bill/Voucher, I is Amount, J is Paid Amount and K is Balance Payable. These column mismatches can understate invoices/costs and misclassify payments. Existing later rows also mix formulas and stored values; no balances have been trusted as production accounting entries.
- Project Record receivable formulas use sale value minus received rather than issued invoices minus verified receipts/credits. Yet-to-invoice and trade receivable must remain distinct in the portal.
- The Guide flags two project dates with year 2206 and maps old Cancelled to Closed. These need source review before import; cancellation is not a signed operational or financial closure.
- The receivables/payables tracker includes borrowing-like labels among receivables. Each proposed opening balance needs classification as customer trade, loan principal or advance before import. The portal keeps loan principal separate.
- The tracker labels its last bucket `90+ Days`, but formulas enter it only above 90. The portal uses `61–90 days` and `91+ days` to make boundaries unambiguous. Its 7/30-day windows include today and overlap.
- Staff sheets include typed duration text and time-of-day intervals that can disagree or cross midnight. Imports need explicit start/end/date mapping and reviewer correction; typed text does not override computed actual hours.

Saving does not review/apply product costs, add recorded material costs again, change inventory/cash/expense/labour/revenue or assign work. ## Training attendance decisions confirmed by user (18 October 2026, 16:46 PKT)

The user has answered the three attendance questions. These decisions supersede earlier entries treating attendance authority/evidence/participant scope as unanswered; do not repeat those questions or interpret them as requiring invented business defaults. There are two types: Client training, linked to a project and usually at handover; and Internal training, for team skills and learning-hours KPI. The facilitator/trainer marks attendance. Client training is then approved by the assigned PM; Internal training is approved by the team lead/HR. Admin may view and correct all, with actual corrections retained in immutable history. No actual facilitator, PM, lead/HR reviewer or participant is assigned by this requirements update.

Required evidence: check-in and check-out time per attendee, entered by the facilitator, plus an uploaded photo or signed attendance sheet. Client training additionally requires portal confirmation by the client's authorized signatory using agreement tick, actual typed name and recorded time, following the charter-style confirmation method. Do not equate the existing text evidence reference, Draft roster membership or general daily staff attendance with these session-specific proofs. No drawn signature or paid e-signature service is requested. Do not silently add a training-PDF generation requirement from the charter feature; the user specified the confirmation method and uploaded evidence here.

Both internal and client/external participants are allowed. Select internal staff from actual user accounts; client/external attendees need only name, phone and email, with no account required. Only internal staff may receive learning-hours/KPI credit; external attendees receive none. Internal training contributes to learning-hours KPI. Retain the existing approved Training activity learning-hours source until an explicit integrated approval/credit path is implemented; prevent duplicate credits and do not award hours from a Draft roster, unapproved attendance or external attendee record. Do not infer breaks, rounding, thresholds, automatic eligibility, extra financial effects or credit for other training types without supported requirements. No external email/SMS/WhatsApp delivery is authorized.

## DEV-026 private attendance proof implementation checkpoint (18 October 2026)

Implemented actual private Photo / Signed attendance sheet uploads against an exact current attendance receipt, retaining its attendee register, actual uploader/server time, safe actual filename, stored size/MIME, manual type/reason and canonical immutable request. UI fields start blank; allowed images PNG/JPEG/WebP and PDF for signed sheets, with the existing 10 MB technical file-size limit. Client checks basic binary signatures and uses a content digest for retry matching; server verifies current source/register/attendance/facilitator authority and actual owned storage metadata. This does not authenticate document contents or approve attendance. Active selected facilitators and administrators upload; authorized coordinators retain historical metadata/download access. Current facilitators see only current-receipt proof. New time/register/source revisions never inherit proof automatically. Metadata CSV is scoped to the receipt; downloads use authenticated private Storage blobs, not public URLs or reusable signed links.

Registered proof bytes/metadata are immutable through application access: no object UPDATE policy or linked-file DELETE permission, no direct metadata IUD. Owners can clean up only their unregistered uploads, including after a source change while still active staff. Registration and orphan cleanup share a path advisory lock; the volatile cleanup guard rechecks fresh metadata after the lock to avoid deletion/registration races. Failed attachment retries preserve the same actual file/path/request without reupload; changed payload gets a new path/identity after safe orphan cleanup. Interrupted binary-upload conflicts require an authenticated download with matching content digest before attachment. Unmounted uploads cannot attach into a changed UI context. Source/receipt/assignment/account changes deny stale attachments and old retries. No production fixtures or actual proof/assignments/activities/costs/learning credit created.

## DEV-025 Client attendance PM review implementation checkpoint (18 October 2026)

Bounded Client-training-only workflow implemented under migration 109. Current selected facilitator or administrator manually selects actual private proof and enters a reason to submit complete recorded attendee time pairs for one exact current Client attendance receipt. Immutable submission snapshots allowlist topic/objective/type/project/definition/version/facilitator, actual attendee identities/contact details, recorded pairs and actual attendance actor/time/reason, plus selected proof metadata. Private coordinator source/history and staff-role snapshots are excluded. Normalized proof foreign keys preserve selected evidence identities. No actual reviewer is assigned automatically; an active account with PM permission and actual current project assignment may approve. Administrators can inspect, return or withdraw current review; approval still requires PM permission plus project assignment. No new self-approval prohibition or business threshold is inferred.

Append-only Approved/Returned/Withdrawn decisions retain actual actor/server time/manual reason/request/previous decision. Exact same-actor retries acknowledge existing receipts; changed payload, stale previous receipt, duplicate/invalid transitions, missing/changed proof or revoked authority are guarded. A submitter may withdraw their own pending submission, but cannot withdraw an approved PM decision. Return/withdrawal permits a new reasoned submission of that receipt; new attendance or source versions make old approvals historical and require a fresh proof/submission/review. Submitted proof is validated against actual private stored objects at submission and approval. Client users gain no visibility/signing rights; no hours, activity/cost, payroll or KPI credit is created. Existing 053 documentation review remains distinct.

HR/Admin manually selects an actual assessment from current independently Approved Pass candidates, inspects exact receipt/review/version/criteria and enters a real issuance reason. Source criteria must be exact latest Active, actual employee and designated assessor current/active, actual independent assigned reviewer eligible and approval unwithdrawn, assessment uncancelled. Draft/unrecorded/unreviewed/Returned/Fail/withdrawn/stale/forged/cancelled/revoked/disabled source authority cannot issue. Assessor, reviewer and ordinary employee gain no proxy issuer power; actual current HR/Admin performs issuance. No global issuer/person is selected automatically.

Issued evidence freezes actual employee/assessor assignment and criteria/checklist, exact recorded Pass receipt and independent review, actual issuer identity/role reference/reason/server time, administrator validity/default 12 months and explicitly configured warning days. No previous certificate, reviewer, issuer, employee, competency or outcome is chosen automatically.

A fresh link requires distinct certificates/assessments for the same actual employee and competency definition, previous issuance no later than new issuance, a valid unrevoked/unexpired new certificate, current exact independently Approved Pass source, active internal employee and current designated assessor/reviewer/current Active criteria. Earlier revoked/expired predecessor evidence can be retained as history without restoring or extending it. Server serialization, exact sequence/request/account checks, one current predecessor/successor and cycle rejection prevent duplicate, stale, forked or circular history. Current HR/Admin may reasonedly withdraw a mistaken link before explicitly correcting it; old link retries only acknowledge the original event and never recreate withdrawn state. Historical cleanup remains possible when the employee/source is no longer eligible.

Both full issued certificate snapshots retain actual assessment/receipt/review/employee/issuer/validity/time/reason evidence. Link-time predecessor/new status, actual linking/withdrawing account, server time, reason and canonical request remain immutable. Neither original issued record, expiry/warning date, revocation nor assessment outcome is rewritten. Linkage does not issue, automatically renew, copy results, extend validity, revoke/restore certificates or award skills/learning/pay/cash. A fresh separately completed assessment, actual independent review and HR/Admin issuance still precede linking. No renewal timing window, grace period or automatic business policy is invented.

Employee-own/HR/Admin RLS, no anonymous read/execute/direct authenticated writes and an immutable history trigger protect this scope. Two guarded RPCs provide exact scoped context/necessary matching predecessor options and reasoned link/withdraw actions. Ordinary assessors/reviewers gain no unrelated certificate access or coordinator directory. Private CSV preserves both snapshots and immutable link/correction evidence. Account/permission/generation/unmount/late-response guards clear failed private data/exports and reconcile uncertain action IDs while preserving unsaved inputs. Full validation, new one-time migration and exact PR/production evidence follow in the release checkpoint. See docs/competence-renewals-guide.md.

## DEV-028 reviewed BOQ scope retirement and linked corrections — 20 October 2026

Bounded implementation: Admin explicitly loads current client publication/decision history for an exact Reviewed original-line proposal, enters an actual reason, checks the scope/history and records immutable retirement. The exact review ID and latest publication ID (or none) must match. Every publication for that proposal must already be Declined or Withdrawn; unresolved Published offers, including expired offers, need explicit withdrawal first. Any Accepted client evidence blocks retirement. Retirement can preserve obsolete/closed-project scope without granting approval or changing the accepted project. The frozen receipt retains the original proposal/review and every publication/decision, actual Admin account, server time and reason.

Retirement prevents further publication/approval of the old Reviewed scope. Existing original submission APIs remain compatible but cannot bypass the explicit correction chain for a retired line. Current assigned PM/Admin selects an actual unconsumed retired predecessor, the exact original accepted line, a current Approved change request/version and manually entered Replace/Remove scope and reason. The corrected proposal inherits only the exact original baseline identity/version/position/content; replacement values remain blank/manual, and no review or client approval is inherited. Its immutable link freezes predecessor/review/retirement and successor evidence, actual account/reason/server time. A Returned correction may be continued from its exact linked predecessor; consumed predecessors cannot branch. Fresh Reviewed corrections may themselves be reasonedly retired subject to the same client guards.

Project-level transaction locks serialize publication, client decision, retirement and correction. Server guards reject revoked/inactive access, stale baseline, stale/withdrawn change approval, unresolved active scope, duplicate/changed retries, wrong review/publication history and branching. Same-account exact retries preserve one immutable event. Correcting retired scope still requires an active project with current accepted source and Approved internal change; retirement of an obsolete project does not relax those gates. Direct API writes and ordinary privileged update/delete cannot rewrite the new history. Current manager-only RLS protects retirement/correction registers and CSV. Client publication projections remain unchanged and exclude this private retirement/correction evidence.

UI: Project BOQ change offers → Original BOQ line revision proposals → load actual project → Reviewed proposal → Check client decisions before BOQ scope retirement → reason/check → Record immutable BOQ scope retirement. Reload, select the exact original accepted line and explicit Correction predecessor, choose the actual Approved request and manually enter new scope/reason, then submit. Retired entries retain original review/history and predecessor/successor evidence, with publication/retirement controls removed. CSV adds retirement reason/account/server time and predecessor/successor IDs. Corrections require fresh internal review and client publication/decision.

No accepted quotation, sale/budget, invoice, credit/refund/tax, workstream/task or assignment was changed. Retirement is not cancellation, and a linked correction is not commercial/execution adoption. No actual business fixtures, assignments, policy values or external messages were inserted/sent.
